Maybe we can solve the full problem via iterative LP:

Detailed LP algorithm:
```
def lp_improve_full(p, budget_s):
    p=normalize_nonneg(p)
    n=p.size
    if n>1200: maybe use cutting subset? full too dense? But let's cap 1024 or 1500.
    S0 = sqrt(2n)
    f = p*S0
    M = max_conv(f)  # since sum S0, score=M
    best_f=f; best_score=M
    start=time; end=start+budget-10
    max_solves based on n: if n<=512 max 40, n<=1024 max 15
    it=0
    while time<end and it<max_solves:
       # optional active subset? Start all constraints. For n=384 all rows.
       A = build_A(f,n)
       b = M * ones(rows)
       # But M changes? Use current M.
       res=linprog(-ones, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit':30})
       if fail: break
       g=res.x; g=clip0; sg=sum(g)
       if sg <= S0*(1+1e-9): maybe no improvement
       g = g*(S0/sg)
       # line search
       best_local = best_score
       # sample t values including maybe 0 (current)
       ts = [1,0.8,0.65,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.25,0.2,0.15,0.1,0.07,0.05,0.03,0.02,0.01]
       for t in ts: h=(1-t)*f + t*g; h=clip0; sh=sum; if sh<=0 continue; h*=S0/sh; sc=max_conv(h)
          if sc<best_local: best_local=sc; best_h=h
       # also maybe golden between best bracket? Could do extra.
       if not improved: break
       f=best_h; M=best_local; best_f...
       # optional small Adam polish? Maybe not.
```

Implementation helpers:
```
def next_power_of_two(m): ...
def fft_convolution(a):
   n=a.size; if n<=direct_limit use np.convolve else L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1); P=rfft(a,L); return np.fft.irfft(P*P,L)[:2*n-1].real
def max_conv_fft(a): c=fft_conv(a); return max(c,0)
def actual_score_fft(a): a=clip0; s=sum; if s<... inf; return 2*n*max_conv_fft(a)/s*s
```

`resample_sequence(p, m)`:
```
def resample_sequence(p, m):
   n=len(p); if m<=1 return [p.sum()]
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   # If inp higher, use average by inverse interpolation? Simple interp may not preserve sum.
   values=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p, left=0.0, right=0.0)
   # For downsampling, interp overestimates? Could instead average over bins.
```
Maybe better: Use `scipy.signal.resample`? Requires scipy. Or simple cumulative sum inverse:
For nonnegative sequence representing step function over [0,1], to resample to m bins, compute cumulative C(p) length n, for new bins cumulative targets t_j=(j+0.5)/m, new p_j = C^{-1}(t_j) - C^{-1}(t_{j-1}). This preserves sum. Implement with `np.interp`:
```
def resample_cdf(p, m):
   a=np.asarray(p); s=a.sum(); c=np.cumsum(a); x_old=np.arange(n)/n? For CDF at bin centers? Simpler continuous: time points 0..1. C at j/m? 
   c = np.concatenate(([0], np.cumsum(a)/s))
   x = np.arange(n+1)/n
   y_new = np.linspace(0,1,m+1)
   vals = np.interp(y_new, x, c)
   return np.diff(vals)
```
This produces nonnegative and sum 1. Good. If m>n, interpolates. If m<n, averages. Use for candidates.

Potential issue: The evaluation sequence length n corresponds to discrete convolution, not continuous CDF resampling exact. But okay as heuristic.

def prune_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a).ravel(); a=clip0
   improved=True
   while improved:
      improved=False
      s=sum(a); if s<... break
      n=len(a)
      if n<=8 break
      # try remove first, last, first+last? evaluate actual_score_fft
      base=actual_score_fft(a)
      for cand in [a[1:], a[:-1], a[1:-1]]:
         sc=actual_score_fft(cand)
         if sc<base-1e-10:
            a=cand; base=sc; improved=True; break
      # maybe threshold small endpoints to zero then trim
   return a
```

Pruning top candidates by iterating remove first/last is safe and may improve. Let's implement a `shrink_search(a)`:
- Start with a (normalized or raw, scale invariant).
- Keep best = a.
- For up to 200 iterations:
   n=len(a); if n<=24 break.
   base=actual_score_fft(a)
   best_candidate=a; best_sc=base
   # Try small margins up to 5 from each end (combinations 120)
   max_margin = min(8, n//4)
   for i in range(max_margin+1):
      for j in range(max_margin+1):
         if i+j==0: continue
         if i+j >= n-2: continue
         cand = a[i:n-j]
         sc = actual_score_fft(cand)
         if sc < best_sc - 1e-9:
             best_sc=sc; best_candidate=cand
   if best_candidate is a: break

Let's implement `fft_conv` carefully:
```
def fft_conv(a, direct_limit=768):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return np.zeros(0)
    if n <= direct_limit:
        return np.convolve(a, a)
    L = next_power_of_two(2*n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2*n-1]
    c = np.real(c)
    # Clean tiny negatives due to roundoff for nonnegative a
    c[c < 0] = 0.0
    return c
```
For nonnegative, true nonnegative. For gradient, we need c maybe with tiny negatives? Clip okay.

`max_conv`:
```
def max_conv(a):
   c=fft_conv(a); return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
```

`actual_score_fft`:
```
def actual_score_fft(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return np.inf
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   n=a.size
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
       return np.inf
   mc=max_conv(a)
   if not np.isfinite(mc): return np.inf
   return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```
This matches evaluator except cap 1000 (we cap sum? enough). For internal normalized sum1, s=1.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(u, floor=1e-300):
   a=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: a=np.ones(16)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   # For optimization, avoid zeros:
   a=np.maximum(a, floor)
   s=a.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: a=np.ones(a.size); s=a.size
   return a/s
```
But if we add floor to large sequence, sum changes negligible. For output, we want zeros allowed? We can use without floor for output. Use separate `soft_positive(u)` for optimization.

`greedy_normalized`:
```
def greedy_normalized(n):
   n=max(2,int(n))
   a=np.empty(n)
   a[0]=1
   for k in range(1,n): a[k]=a[k-1]*(2*k-1)/(2*k)
   return normalize_nonneg(a)
```
This can overflow? a~1/sqrt(k) no. Use iterative.

`power_normalized(n,s)`: use `idx=np.arange(n)+1; a=idx**(-s); normalize`.

`resample_cdf(a,m)`:
```
def resample_cdf(a,m):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.ones(m)/m
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(m)/m
   x=np.arange(n+1,dtype=np.float64)/float(n)
   c=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(a)/s))
   y=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m+1)
   vals=np.interp(y,x,c)
   out=np.diff(vals)
   out=np.clip(out,0.0,None)
   return normalize_nonneg(out)
```
This preserves sum before normalize. Good.

Adam v2:
```
def adam_v2(p, budget_s, alpha=100.0, lr=0.03, mode='lse', hard_frac=0.0, seed=0):
   p=soft_normalize(p)
   n=p.size
   if budget_s < 5: return p, actual_score_fft(p)
   x=np.log(p); x-=x.mean(); x=np.clip(x,-80,80); x-=x.mean()
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   best_p=p.copy(); best_f=score_sum1(p)
   start=time.monotonic(); end=start+max(0,budget_s-2)
   # We can vary alpha? Use fixed. Maybe decay lr.
   while time.monotonic() < end:
      t+=1
      elapsed=time.monotonic()-start; frac=min(1,elapsed/(end-start+1))
      # lr_t = lr*(1 - 0.5*frac)
      if mode=='hard' or hard_frac>0:
         w, maxc, lse_val = hard_weights(p, hard_frac)
      else:
         w, maxc, lse_val = lse_weights(p, alpha)
      # value = 2*n*lse_val
      val = 2.0*n*lse_val
      if finite and val<best_f: best...
      B = grad_convolution(p,w)
      grad_p = 2.0*n*B  # score gradient wrt a
      gp = np.dot(p, grad_p)
      grad_x = p*(grad_p - gp)
      # optional NaN
      if not finite: break
      # Adam update
      m = 0.9*m +0.1*grad_x
      v = 0.999*v+0.001*grad_x*grad_x
      mhat=m/(1-0.9**t); vhat=v/(1-0.999**t)
      step = lr_t * mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
      step=np.clip(step,-10,10)
      x = x - step
      x -= x.mean()
      x = np.clip(x,-80,80); x-=x.mean(); x-=x.max()
      e=np.exp(x); se=e.sum(); if not finite break; p=e/se
   finalf=score_sum1(p); if < best: update
   return best_p,best_f
```
Need `score_sum1(p)`: since p sum1, `2*n*max_conv(p)`. But due sum maybe not exactly 1, use actual_score_fft? For speed use:
```
def score_sum1(a):
  n=a.size; s=a.sum(); if s<=0: inf; return 2*n*max_conv(a)/(s*s)
```
Works.

def lse_weights(a, alpha):

 z=alpha*(c-maxc); mask=z>-50; exp=np.zeros_like(c); exp[mask]=np.exp(z[mask]); ssum=exp.sum(); if ssum<=0: idx=argmax(c); w[idx]=1; lse=maxc; else w=exp/ssum; lse=maxc + np.log(ssum)/alpha; return w,maxc,lse
```

def hard_weights(a, frac):
 c=fft_conv(a); maxc=max; num=c.size
 k=max(1,int(np.ceil(frac*num)))
 # top k via partition
 if k>=num: idx=np.arange(num)
 else:
    part=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    idx=part
 w=zeros; w[idx]=1/k
 # For value use max? For hard objective use maxc or average top? Use maxc as value. But gradient of max is uniform active. lse_val=maxc.
 return w,maxc,maxc
```

Potential gradient extraction function:
```
def grad_convolution(a,w):
   n=a.size; L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
   P=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   # Need w length 2n-1. Ensure.
   W=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], L)
   y=np.fft.irfft(P*W, L)
   # B_i = y[n-1-i]
   B = y[n-1:-n-1:-1]  # n-1 down to 0
   # Check length: y[n-1:-n-1:-1] includes n-1,...,0
   B = np.real(B)
   B[B<0]=0
   return B
```
Let's verify slice in Python: For n=3, y length L. `y[2:-4:-1]` gives indices 2,1,0. Good. For n=1, `y[0:-2:-1]` gives [0]? In Python, y[0:-2:-1] with stop -2 (i.e. L-2) going negative step? It may produce [0] if 0 >= L-2? For L maybe 1? next power 1? For n=1, 2n-1=1, L=1. `y[0:-1:-1]`? I need safe. Use explicit array: `idx = np.arange(n-1, -1, -1); B = y[idx].real`. This works for n=1 idx [0]. Good.

Need ensure w length exactly 2n-1. If c length from fft_conv = 2n-1. Good.

We need `linprog` availability. Build A:
```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
   n=f.size; rows=2*n-1; A=np.zeros((rows,n),float)
   for k in range(rows):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
      if lo<=hi:
         A[k, lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   return A
```
Check for k=0: lo=0 hi=0; f[0-0:0-0+1][::-1]=f[0:1]=f0, row g0 coeff f0. c0=f0*g0 correct.
k=1: lo0 hi1; f[1-1:1-0+1]=f[0:2][::-1]=[f1,f0]; row g0 f1, g1 f0. c1=f1 g0 + f0 g1 correct.
Good.

`lp_improve` function:

def lp_improve(p, budget_s, seed=0, max_iterations=None, relax_schedule=True):
   p=soft_normalize(p) # for LP we can have zeros? Use normalize with floor? LP accepts zero. Use nonneg floor maybe.
   n=p.size
   if n<2 or n>1300 or budget_s<30 or scipy_opt is None: return p, actual_score_fft(p)
   S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
   f=p*S0
   M=actual_score_fft(f)  # since sum S0, should equal max_conv
   # M maybe score; with sum S0 exact? actual_score_fft caps etc. Use max_conv(f) better.
   M=max_conv(f)
   if not finite: return
   best_f=f.copy(); best_score=M
   end=time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s-20)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   it=0
   stall=0
   relax=1.0
   max_iter = 60 if n<=512 else (30 if n<=1024 else 15)
   while time<end and it<max_iter:
      it+=1
      A=build_conv_matrix(f)
      b=np.full(2*n-1, M*relax, dtype=float)
      # objective: mostly maximize sum, sometimes random
      if it % 5 == 0:
         cobj = -np.ones(n)
      else:
         cobj = -np.ones(n)
         # small noise to explore
         cobj += 0.05 * rng.normal(size=n)
         # ensure not too positive? fine
      res=None
      tl = min(60.0, max(5.0, (end-time.monotonic())*0.4))
      try: res=scipy_opt.linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0,None), method='highs', options={'time_limit': float(tl)})
      except: break
      if res is None or not res.success or res.x is None: break
      g=np.asarray(res.x,float); g=np.clip(g,0,None); sg=float(np.sum(g))
      if not finite or sg<=1e-12: break
      # If sg < S0, still maybe use but with low priority. We can try anyway.
      g = g * (S0 / sg)
      # line search
      h, sc = line_search_fp(f,g,S0)
      if sc < best_score*(1-1e-12):
          best_score=sc; best_f=h.copy(); f=h; M=sc; relax=1.0; stall=0
      else:
          stall+=1
          if stall==2: relax = min(1.2, relax*1.1)
          elif stall>=5: break
          # also if no improvement, maybe f unchanged but M maybe relax? We keep f best.
          # To allow next LP direction, maybe use current best f, not worse.
   # final best_f
   return normalize_nonneg(best_f), best_score
```

def line_search_fp(f,g,S0):
   # f,g 1D sum S0
   best_h=f.copy(); best_sc=max_conv(f)
   # grid
   ts=[0.0,0.01,0.02,0.03,0.05,0.08,0.12,0.18,0.25,0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65,0.75,0.85,0.95,1.0]
   for t in ts:
       if t==0: sc=best_sc; h=f
       else:
           h=(1-t)*f+t*g; h=np.clip(h,0,None); sh=sum; if sh>0: h*=S0/sh
           sc=max_conv(h)
       if sc<best_sc: best...
   # local refine around best_t if 0<t<1: step size 0.02
   if 0<best_t<1:
      low=max(0,best_t-0.05); high=min(1,best_t+0.05)
      for t in np.linspace(low,high,21): evaluate
   return best_h,best_sc
```

class LBFGSProblem:
    def __init__(self, alpha):
        self.alpha = alpha
        self.best_p = None
        self.best_f = np.inf
    def value_grad(self, x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size == 0: return [np.inf, np.zeros(0)]
        x = np.clip(x, -100, 100)
        x = x - x.max()
        e = np.exp(x)
        s = e.sum()
        if not finite: ... fallback
        a = e/s
        n=a.size
        c=fft_conv(a); maxc=max
        z=alpha*(c-maxc); mask...; w...
        lse=maxc+logsum/alpha
        f=2*n*lse
        B=grad_convolution(a,w)
        grad_a=4*n*B? Wait score gradient with alpha: d score/da =2n*∑ w_k dc_k/da =2n*2B =4nB. Yes.
        dot=np.dot(a, grad_a)
        grad_x=a*(grad_a - dot)
        # track best exact? Could track smooth.
        return f, grad_x
    def fun(self,x):
        f,g=self.value_grad(x); self.current_f=f; self.current_g=g; return f
    def jac(self,x):
        f,g=self.value_grad(x); return g
```

def value_grad(self,x): compute store self._f,self._g
def fun(self,x): self._compute(x); return self._f
def jac(self,x): if self._f is None or not np.array_equal(self._x,x): self._compute(x); return self._g
```
Ok.

def best_trim_sequence(a, max_margin=48, max_evals=2500):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel(); a=np.clip(a,0,None)
    n=a.size
    if n<4: return a, actual_score_fft(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=actual_score_fft(a)
    evals=1
    max_margin=min(max_margin, n//3)
    # first greedy iterative local
    # exhaustive margins: for i in range(0,max_margin+1): for j in range(0,max_margin+1): if i+j==0 continue; if n-i-j < 4 continue
        cand=a[i:n-j]; sc=actual_score_fft(cand)
        if sc<best_sc: update
    # After exhaustive, maybe shrink best further with larger margins? Could repeat once on best window with margins around current.
    # Also try thresholding extremely small values inside? Not.
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Let's implement correct FFT gradient:
```
def grad_convolution(a, w):
    n = a.size
    w = np.asarray(w,float); a_rev = a[::-1]
    # direct if small
    if n <= 256:
       C = np.convolve(w, a_rev)
       return C[n-1:2*n-1].copy()
    L = next_power_of_two((2*n-1) + n - 1)  # 3n-2
    Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    Fa = np.fft.rfft(a_rev, L)
    C = np.fft.irfft(Fw * Fa, L)
    B = C[n-1:2*n-1].real
    B[B<0]=0
    return B
```

def hard_weights(a, frac):
 c=fft_conv(a); maxc=...
 k=2*n-1
 if frac <= 0:
    idx=np.where(c >= maxc - max(1e-12, 1e-9*maxc))[0]
    if idx.size==0: idx=[argmax]
 else:
    kmax=max(1,int(np.ceil(frac*c.size)))
    if kmax>=c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size)
    else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -kmax)[-kmax:]
 w zeros; w[idx]=1/idx.size; return w,maxc,maxc
```

def select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, k, max_len=None, min_len=None):
   items=[]
   if best_p is not None: items.append((best_score, int(best_p.size), best_p))
   for n,rec in best_by_n.items():
      if max_len is not None and n>max_len: continue
      if min_len is not None and n<min_len: continue
      items.append((rec[0], int(n), rec[1]))
   # sort by score
   items.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
   # unique by n? If best_p duplicates, remove
   unique=[]; seen=set()
   for sc,n,p in items:
      if n in seen: continue
      seen.add(n); unique.append((sc,n,p))
      if len(unique)>=k: break
   return unique
```

def geometric_decay(n, beta, direction=0):
   idx=np.arange(n)
   if direction==0: a=np.exp(-beta*idx/(n-1))
   else: a=np.exp(-beta*(n-1-idx)/(n-1))

`try_global_eval(seq)`:
```
def try_global_eval(seq):
   try:
      val = evaluate_sequence(list(seq))
   except Exception:
      return np.inf
   try: val=float(val)
   except: return np.inf
   return val if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf
```

`scale_output(p,target_sum=10.0)`:
```
def scale_output(p,target_sum=10.0):
   a=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
   if a.size==0: a=np.ones(16)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not finite or s <= 0: a=np.ones(a.size); s=a.size
   # target 10 safe
   a=a*(target_sum/s)
   # if any >1000, scale down
   amax=float(a.max())
   if amax>1000.0:
       a=a*(1000.0/amax)
   s2=float(a.sum())
   if s2<0.01: a=a*(0.01/s2)
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```
Since scale invariant, target 10 okay. If after cap sum changes? For our small values no cap.

Detailed functions:

```
import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as scipy_opt
except Exception:
    scipy_opt = None

def tuple_score_key(x): return x[0]

def next_power_of_two(m):
    L=1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L

def fft_conv(a, direct_limit=256):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n=a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0,dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= direct_limit:
        c=np.convolve(a,a)
    else:
        L=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
        fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
        c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,L)[:2*n-1]
        c=c.real
        c[c < 0.0]=0.0
    # For direct also maybe no negative but clip
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    c[c < 0.0]=0.0
    return c

def max_conv(a): ...
def actual_score_fft(a): ...
def score_sum1(a): return actual_score_fft(a) # but can optimize
```
`score_sum1` can call actual_score_fft; for speed in Adam, we compute exact from maxc. For record, actual.

`normalize_nonneg`:
```
def normalize_nonneg(u, floor=0.0):
    a=np.asarray(u,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
       a=np.ones(16,dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if floor > 0:
       a=np.maximum(a, floor)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
       a=np.ones(a.size,dtype=np.float64)
       s=float(a.size)
    return a/s
```
If floor >0 and a was all zeros, becomes floor and normalized to uniform.

`soft_normalize`: `return normalize_nonneg(u, floor=1e-12)`.

Greedy:
```
def greedy_normalized(n):
   n=max(int(n),2)
   a=np.empty(n,float); a[0]=1.0
   for k in range(1,n): a[k]=a[k-1]*(2.0*k-1.0)/(2.0*k)
   return normalize_nonneg(a)
```
Could use `math`? no.

`power_normalized`:
```
def power_normalized(n,s):
   n=max(int(n),2)
   idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
   a=np.power(idx, -float(s))
   return normalize_nonneg(a)
```

`geometric_normalized(n,beta,reverse=False)`:
```
idx=np.arange(n); if reverse: idx=n-1-idx; a=np.exp(-float(beta)*idx/(n-1)); normalize
```

`mix_greedy_uniform`:
```
lambda? no: a=lam*greedy+(1-lam)*ones; normalize
```

`perturb_greedy(n,rng,sigma)`:
```
p=greedy_normalized(n); noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,n)); return normalize_nonneg(p*noise, floor=1e-12)
```

`record_candidate`:
```
def record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, score=None):
   a=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size ==0: return best_p,best_score
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   if score is None: score=actual_score_fft(a)
   if not np.isfinite(score): return best_p,best_score
   if score < best_score:
      best_p=a.copy(); best_score=float(score)
   n=int(a.size)
   old=best_by_n.get(n)
   if old is None or score < old[0]:
      best_by_n[n]=(float(score), a.copy())
   return best_p,best_score
```
Note: If p is not normalized (e.g., LP returns raw sum S0?), actual_score handles. We usually record normalized sum1.

`select_top` as above.

`get_lse_weights`:
```
def get_lse_weights(a, alpha):
   c=fft_conv(a); n=a.size; maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   if maxc <=0 or alpha <=1:
      w=np.full(c.size, 1.0/c.size) if c.size else np.zeros(0); return w,maxc,maxc
   z=alpha*(c-maxc)
   mask = z > -50.0
   expz=np.zeros_like(c)
   np.exp(z[mask], out=expz[mask])
   ssum=float(np.sum(expz))
   if not finite or ssum <=0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros_like(c); w[idx]=1.0; lse=maxc
   else:
      w=expz/ssum; lse=maxc + np.log(ssum)/alpha
   return w,maxc,lse
```
If alpha small, uniform maybe; but alpha >1.

`get_hard_weights`:
```
def get_hard_weights(a, frac):
   c=fft_conv(a); maxc=...
   size=c.size; w=np.zeros(size)
   if frac is None or frac <=0:
      tol=max(1e-12, 1e-8*maxc)
      idx=np.where(c >= maxc - tol)[0]
      if idx.size ==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
   else:
      k=int(np.ceil(float(frac)*float(size)))
      k=max(1,min(size,k))
      if k >= size: idx=np.arange(size)
      else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
   w[idx] = 1.0/float(idx.size)
   return w,maxc,maxc
```

`grad_convolution` as above. Use direct for n<=128 maybe:
```
def grad_convolution(a,w):
   n=a.size
   if n == 0: return np.zeros(0)
   a_rev=a[::-1]
   if n <= 128:
      C=np.convolve(w, a_rev)
      B=C[n-1:2*n-1]
   else:
      L=next_power_of_two((2*n-1)+(n-1))
      Fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
      Fa=np.fft.rfft(a_rev,L)
      C=np.fft.irfft(Fw*Fa,L)
      B=C[n-1:2*n-1]
   B=np.asarray(B,dtype=np.float64).real
   B[B<0]=0.0
   return B
```
Check slice `C[n-1:2*n-1]`: For n=2, C length 3, slice [1:3] indices 1,2. good. For n=1, [0:1]. good.

`adam_v2` as planned. Need ensure `a_rev` in grad direct uses w length 2n-1 and a_rev length n, conv length 3n-2; slice exists.

`lp_improve` as planned. Need `build_conv_matrix`. For performance, if n large, building dense each solve can be memory/time. We'll cap.

```
def build_conv_matrix(f):
   n=f.size; rows=2*n-1
   A=np.zeros((rows,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for k in range(rows):
      lo = k - n + 1
      if lo < 0: lo = 0
      hi = k
      if hi >= n: hi = n-1
      if lo <= hi:
          A[k, lo:hi+1] = f[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   return A
```
Check slice `f[k-hi:k-lo+1]`: For k=4,n=3: lo=2,hi=2; f[4-2:4-2+1]=f[2:3], reversed f2. row g2 coeff f2. c4=f2*g2 yes. For k=3: lo=1,hi=2; f[1:3][::-1]=[f2,f1]; j=1 coeff f2, j=2 coeff f1. c3=f2*g1+f1*g2? Wait c3 = ∑ f_i g_{3-i}: i=1 f1*g2, i=2 f2*g1. Row j=1 should f2, j=2 f1. correct.

`line_search_params(f,g,S0)`:
```
def line_search_params(f,g,S0):
   f=np.asarray(f,float); g=np.asarray(g,float)
   best_h=f.copy(); best_sc=max_conv(f); best_t=0.0
   # grid list
   for t in (0.0,0.01,0.02,0.03,0.05,0.08,0.12,0.18,0.25,0.35,0.45,0.55,0.65,0.75,0.85,0.95,1.0):
       if t == 0.0:
          h=f; sc=best_sc
       else:
          h=(1.0-t)*f + t*g
          h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
          sh=float(np.sum(h))
          if sh <= 0: continue
          h=h*(S0/sh)
          sc=max_conv(h)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
          best_sc=sc; best_h=h.copy(); best_t=t
   if 0.0 < best_t < 1.0:
      lo=max(0.0,best_t-0.05); hi=min(1.0,best_t+0.05)
      for m in range(11):
         t=lo + (hi-lo)*m/10.0
         if abs(t-best_t) < 1e-6: continue
         h=(1-t)*f+t*g; ...
   return best_h,best_sc,best_t
```

```
def lp_improve(p, budget_s, seed=0, max_solves=None):
   p = soft_normalize(p, floor=1e-12)
   n=p.size
   if scipy_opt is None or n < 8 or n > 1100 or budget_s < 40:
       return p, actual_score_fft(p)
   S0=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
   f = p*S0
   M = max_conv(f)
   if not finite: return...
   best_f=f.copy(); best_score=M
   end=time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s-30)
   if max_solves is None:
       if n <= 512: max_solves=35
       else: max_solves=18
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   it=0; stall=0; relax=1.0
   ub = 10.0*S0
   bounds = [(0.0, ub) for _ in range(n)]
   while time < end and it < max_solves:
       it += 1
       A = build_conv_matrix(f)
       b = np.full(2*n-1, M*relax, dtype=np.float64)
       if it % 4 == 0:
           cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
       else:
           noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
           cobj = -np.ones(n) - 0.03*noise
       rem = end - time.monotonic()
       if rem < 8: break
       tl = min(45.0, max(3.0, rem*0.35))
       res=None
       try:
           res=scipy_opt.linprog(cobj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'time_limit': float(tl)})
       except Exception:
           break
       if res is None or not getattr(res,'success',False) or getattr(res,'x',None) is None:
           # Try smaller time? break
           break
       g=np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       g=np.maximum(g,0.0); sg=float(np.sum(g))
       if not np.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1e-12: break
       g = g*(S0/sg)
       h, sc, t_best = line_search_params(f,g,S0)
       if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score*(1-1e-12):
           best_score=float(sc); best_f=h; f=h; M=best_score; relax=1.0; stall=0
           print("LP improve n=%d it=%d score=%.6f t=%.3f" % (n,it,best_score,t_best))
       else:
           stall += 1
           if stall == 2:
               relax = min(1.25, relax*1.08)
           elif stall >= 5:
               break
   # final normalized
   out = best_f / S0
   return soft_normalize(out, floor=0.0)? Actually floor 0. Use normalize_nonneg(out, floor=0.0)
```

`lbfgs_polish` class and function.

Class top-level:
```
class LBFGSProblem:
    def __init__(self, alpha, floor=1.0):
       self.alpha = float(alpha)
       self._f = None
       self._g = None
       self._x = None
    def _compute(self,x):
       x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if x.size == 0:
           self._f=np.inf; self._g=np.zeros(0); self._x=x; return
       x=np.clip(x,-100.0,100.0)
       xm=x-x.max()
       e=np.exp(xm)
       s=float(e.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
           a=np.ones(x.size)/x.size
       else:
           a=e/s
       n=a.size
       c=fft_conv(a); maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
       if maxc <=0:
          self._f=0.0; self._g=np.zeros(n); self._x=x; return
       z=self.alpha*(c-maxc)
       mask=z > -50.0
       expz=np.zeros_like(c)
       np.exp(z[mask], out=expz[mask])
       ssum=float(expz.sum())
       if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <=0:
           w=np.zeros_like(c); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; lse=maxc
       else:
           w=expz/ssum; lse=maxc+np.log(ssum)/self.alpha
       fval=float(2.0*n*lse)
       B=grad_convolution(a,w)
       grad_p=4.0*n*B
       dot=float(np.dot(a,grad_p))
       grad_x=a*(grad_p-dot)
       grad_x=np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)
       self._f=float(fval); self._g=grad_x; self._x=x
    def fun(self,x):
       if self._x is None or not np.array_equal(self._x, np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()):
           self._compute(x)
       return self._f
    def jac(self,x):
       if self._x is None or not np.array_equal(self._x, np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()):
           self._compute(x)
       return self._g
```
This uses `np.array_equal` each call, okay. No nested functions.

`lbfgs_polish(p,budget_s,alpha=80,seed=0)`:
```
def lbfgs_polish(p, budget_s, alpha=80.0, seed=0):
   if scipy_opt is None or budget_s < 20: return p, actual_score_fft(p)
   p=soft_normalize(p, floor=1e-12)
   n=p.size
   if n<4 or n>2500: return p, actual_score_fft(p)
   best_p=p.copy(); best_f=actual_score_fft(p)
   x=np.log(p); x-=x.mean(); x=np.clip(x,-60,60); x-=x.mean()
   prob=LBFGSProblem(alpha)
   start=time.monotonic(); end=start+budget_s
   # Determine maxiter
   if n <= 256: mi=20
   elif n <=512: mi=15
   elif n<=1024: mi=10
   else: mi=5
   while time.monotonic() < end - 5:
       try:
           res=scipy_opt.minimize(prob.fun, x, jac=prob.jac, method='L-BFGS-B',
                                  options={'maxiter': mi, 'maxls': 20, 'gtol': 1e-8})
       except Exception:
           break
       if hasattr(res,'x') and res.x is not None:
           x=np.asarray(res.x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
           # convert to p
           x=np.clip(x,-100,100); x-=x.max(); e=np.exp(x); s=e.sum()
           if finite s>0:
              p_new=e/s
              f_new=actual_score_fft(p_new)
              if np.isfinite(f_new) and f_new < best_f:
                  best_f=f_new; best_p=p_new.copy()
        # If res success? not needed. Continue if time.
   return best_p,best_f
```
Potential issue: `np.log(p)` if p has zeros due floor? soft floor >0. Good.

`x` may have large mean; after res, shift.

L-BFGS objective smooth value may not decrease if alpha large. Use alpha 60. We'll call with alpha=60 maybe. Could also call alpha=150 for final. We'll decide.

`best_trim_sequence`:
```
def best_trim_sequence(a, max_margin=48, max_evals=3000):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: return a, np.inf
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   n=a.size
   if n<6:
      sc=actual_score_fft(a); return a, sc
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=actual_score_fft(a)
   evals=1
   max_margin=min(int(max_margin), n//3)
   if max_margin < 1:
       return best_a,best_sc
   # We can reduce max_margin if many evals expected
   max_margin=min(max_margin, int(np.sqrt(max_evals/2)))
   for i in range(max_margin+1):
       for j in range(max_margin+1):
           if evals >= max_evals: break
           if i == 0 and j == 0: continue
           new_len = n - i - j
           if new_len < 6: continue
           cand = a[i:n-j]
           sc = actual_score_fft(cand)
           evals += 1
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
               best_sc = sc; best_a = cand.copy()
       if evals >= max_evals: break
   # second pass on best_a with smaller margin to refine
   n2=best_a.size
   if n2 - 2*i? We don't know trim amounts. Could just do greedy remove one end while improves.
   improved=True
   while improved and best_a.size > 6:
       improved=False
       base=best_sc
       for cand in (best_a[1:], best_a[:-1]):
           if cand.size < 6: continue
           sc=actual_score_fft(cand)
           if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < base - 1e-12:
               best_a=cand; best_sc=sc; improved=True; break
   return best_a,best_sc
```
The second greedy might remove one each iter; maybe up to 10. Fine.

But exhaustive with max_margin=48 and n=2048: max_eval=3000 => sqrt(1500)=38, less. Good.

Potential performance with direct_limit=256 means for n=384, actual_score uses FFT. Many calls okay. Could set `fft_conv` direct_limit=128 in pruning to use FFT more? Already 256. For n=300, direct maybe 90k, same. Fine.

Now, test correctness mentally for small n.

`fft_conv` with direct_limit=256: for n=2 direct. Clipping small negative okay.

`actual_score_fft([1,1])`: a sum2, n2, conv [1,2,1], max2, score=2*2*2/4=2. Good.

Adam gradient for n=2, suppose p [0.5,0.5], c [0.25,0.5,0.25], maxc=0.5, lse alpha weights top mostly lag1. B for lag1 = [a1,a0]=[0.5,0.5], grad_p=4nB=8*[0.5,0.5] (n=2 =>4*2=8? wait 4*n=8), grad_x zero (symmetric). If p not symmetric e.g., [0.6,0.4], c max lag1? B=[0.4,0.6], grad_p positive more for a0? It will reduce? If max lag1, derivative wrt a0=2B0? Actually score derivative =4nB, B from w; for lag1, B=[a1,a0], grad_p=[8a1,8a0]. If a0=0.6,a1=0.4, grad_p0=3.2, grad_p1=4.8; dot=0.6*3.2+0.4*4.8=4.0; grad_x0=0.6*(3.2-4)=-0.48, grad_x1=0.4*(4.8-4)=0.32. Update x reduces a0? Negative grad_x => x increases? We do x = x - lr*step. Adam step approx grad_x. x0 decreases? Wait gradient ascent? We minimize, x = x - step. For p=softmax(x), gradient grad_x of objective; step = m/(sqrt v). If grad_x negative for x0, step negative, x = x - negative = increase x0, increasing a0. But gradient wrt x0 negative means increasing x0 decreases objective? Let's check objective max c1=2a0a1 under sum1. If a0=0.6,a1=0.4, c1=0.48; if increase a0 to 0.65,a1=0.35, c1=0.455 decreases. So objective decreases when x0 increases. Gradient should be negative (d objective/dx0 negative). x = x - lr*grad: if grad negative, x increases. Correct. Good.

For c0 max (a0^2), derivative wrt x0? If max c0, want increase a0? Actually if a0 dominant, c0 high; to reduce c0, decrease a0 (increase x1). w c0, B for c0: B0=a0, B1=0? gradient_p=4n*a0 for a0, 0 for a1; dot=4n a0^2; grad_x0=4n a0^2 positive, grad_x1=-4n a0 a1? x step decreases x0, increases x1. Good.

Thus gradient correct.

Potential bug in `select_top`: If best_p is not None with score np.inf? record only finite. okay.

```
def select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, k, max_len=None, min_len=None):
    items=[]
    if best_p is not None and np.isfinite(best_score):
        items.append((float(best_score), int(best_p.size), best_p))
    for n, rec in best_by_n.items():
        n=int(n)
        if max_len is not None and n > max_len: continue
        if min_len is not None and n < min_len: continue
        items.append((float(rec[0]), n, rec[1]))
    if not items: return []
    items.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
    out=[]; seen=set()
    for item in items:
       n=item[1]
       if n in seen: continue
       seen.add(n); out.append(item)
       if len(out)>=k: break
    return out
```
If best_p length duplicate in items from best_by_n, seen may skip best_if duplicate? It will first item best_p (maybe best) with n; then skip other same n. okay.

`random_perturb(p, rng, sigma)`:
```
def random_perturb(p, rng, sigma):
   p=soft_normalize(p, floor=1e-12)
   noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), p.size))
   return soft_normalize(p*noise, floor=1e-12)
```

```
def geometric_normalized(n, beta, reverse=False):
   n=max(int(n),2)
   idx=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
   if reverse: idx = n-1.0-idx
   a=np.exp(-float(beta)*idx/(float(n)-1.0))
   return normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative step-function coefficient sequence minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

by combining:
  - cheap FFT-based evaluations,
  - several structured starts (greedy sqrt(1/(1-x)), power laws, geometric decays,
    resampling of the supplied best-known sequence),
  - smoothed subgradient descent in log-probability coordinates,
  - cutting-plane / all-constraint LP directions inspired by the previous SOTA,
  - optional L-BFGS polishing of the smoothed objective,
  - exhaustive trimming of prefix/suffix margins, since changing the length is
    part of the search.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

try:
    import scipy.optimize as scipy_opt
except Exception:
    scipy_opt = None


def tuple_score_key(x):
    return x[0]


def next_power_of_two(m):
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def fft_conv(a, direct_limit=256):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= direct_limit:
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
    else:
        L = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        c = c.real
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def max_conv(a):
    c = fft_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0
    return float(np.max(c))


def actual_score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    mc = max_conv(a)
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * mc / (s * s))


def normalize_nonneg(u, floor=0.0):
    a = np.asarray(u, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def soft_normalize(u, floor=1e-12):
    return normalize_nonneg(u, floor=floor)


def greedy_normalized(n):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
    return normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)


def power_normalized(n, s):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.power(idx, -float(s))
    return normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)


def geometric_normalized(n, beta, reverse=False):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if reverse:
        idx = float(n - 1) - idx
    a = np.exp(-float(beta) * idx / float(n - 1))
    return normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)


def mix_greedy_uniform(n, lam):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    lam = float(lam)
    a = lam * greedy_normalized(n) + (1.0 - lam) * np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_nonneg(a, floor=0.0)


def perturb_greedy(n, rng, sigma):
    p = greedy_normalized(n)
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), p.size))
    return soft_normalize(p * noise, floor=1e-12)


def random_perturb(p, rng, sigma):
    p = soft_normalize(p, floor=1e-12)
    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), p.size))
    return soft_normalize(p * noise, floor=1e-12)


def resample_cdf(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = max(int(m), 1)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(n)
    x = np.arange(n + 1, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    c = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(a) / s))
    y = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m + 1)
    vals = np.interp(y, x, c)
    out = np.diff(vals)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, None)
    return normalize_nonneg(out, floor=0.0)


def prepare_start(n, kind, rng=None):
    n = max(int(n), 2)
    if kind == "greedy":
        return greedy_normalized(n)
    if kind == "power45":
        return power_normalized(n, 0.45)
    if kind == "power50":
        return power_normalized(n, 0.50)
    if kind == "power55":
        return power_normalized(n, 0.55)
    if kind == "mix25":
        return mix_greedy_uniform(n, 0.25)
    if kind == "mix50":
        return mix_greedy_uniform(n, 0.50)
    if kind == "mix75":
        return mix_greedy_uniform(n, 0.75)
    if kind == "geom1":
        return geometric_normalized(n, 1.0, reverse=False)
    if kind == "geom2":
        return geometric_normalized(n, 1.0, reverse=True)
    if kind == "geom20":
        return geometric_normalized(n, 2.0, reverse=False)
    if rng is not None:
        if kind == "pert05":
            return perturb_greedy(n, rng, 0.05)
        if kind == "pert15":
            return perturb_greedy(n, rng, 0.15)
        if kind == "pert25":
            return perturb_greedy(n, rng, 0.25)
    return greedy_normalized(n)


def record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, score=None):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_p, best_score
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if score is None:
        score = actual_score_fft(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return best_p, best_score
    if score < best_score:
        best_p = a.copy()
        best_score = float(score)
    n = int(a.size)
    old = best_by_n.get(n)
    if old is None or score < old[0]:
        best_by_n[n] = (float(score), a.copy())
    return best_p, best_score


def select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, k, max_len=None, min_len=None):
    items = []
    if best_p is not None and np.isfinite(best_score):
        items.append((float(best_score), int(best_p.size), best_p))
    for n, rec in best_by_n.items():
        n = int(n)
        if max_len is not None and n > max_len:
            continue
        if min_len is not None and n < min_len:
            continue
        items.append((float(rec[0]), n, rec[1]))
    if not items:
        return []
    items.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
    out = []
    seen = set()
    for item in items:
        n = item[1]
        if n in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(n)
        out.append(item)
        if len(out) >= k:
            break
    return out


def get_lse_weights(a, beta):
    c = fft_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 0.0
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 1e-300:
        w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return w, maxc, maxc
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1.0:
        w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return w, maxc, maxc
    z = beta * (c / maxc - 1.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
    expz = np.exp(z)
    ssum = float(np.sum(expz))
    if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros_like(c)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w, maxc, maxc
    w = expz / ssum
    lse = maxc * (1.0 + float(np.log(ssum)) / beta)
    return w, maxc, lse


def get_hard_weights(a, frac):
    c = fft_conv(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 0.0
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    size = c.size
    if frac is None or frac <= 0.0:
        tol = max(1e-14, 1e-8 * maxc)
        idx = np.where(c >= maxc - tol)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
    else:
        k = int(np.ceil(float(frac) * float(size)))
        k = max(1, min(size, k))
        if k >= size:
            idx = np.arange(size, dtype=int)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([0], dtype=int)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(idx.size)
    return w, maxc, maxc


def grad_convolution(a, w):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    a_rev = a[::-1]
    if n <= 128:
        c = np.convolve(w, a_rev)
        b = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    else:
        L = next_power_of_two((2 * n - 1) + (n - 1))
        fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a_rev, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fw * fa, L)
        b = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).real
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    return b


def adam_v2(p, budget_s, beta=80.0, lr=0.035, hard_frac=0.0, seed=0):
    p = soft_normalize(p, floor=1e-12)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s <= 1.5:
        return p, actual_score_fft(p)

    x = np.log(p)
    x -= x.mean()
    x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 60.0)
    x -= x.mean()

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_f = actual_score_fft(p)

    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 1.5)
    base_den = max(1e-9, budget_s - 1.5)

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        t += 1
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / base_den)

        if hard_frac is not None and hard_frac > 0.0:
            w, maxc, _ = get_hard_weights(p, hard_frac)
        else:
            w, maxc, _ = get_lse_weights(p, beta)

        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 1e-300:
            break

        exact = float(2.0 * n * maxc)
        if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < best_f:
            best_f = exact
            best_p = p.copy()

        b = grad_convolution(p, w)
        grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * b
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_p)):
            break
        dot_pg = float(np.dot(p, grad_p))
        grad_x = p * (grad_p - dot_pg)
        grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)

        b1, b2, eps = 0.9, 0.999, 1e-8
        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad_x
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad_x * grad_x)
        mhat = m / (1.0 - b1 ** t)
        vhat = v / (1.0 - b2 ** t)
        step = (lr * max(0.15, 1.0 - 0.75 * frac)) * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)
        step = np.clip(step, -6.0, 6.0)

        x = x - step
        x -= x.mean()
        x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 60.0)
        x -= x.mean()
        x = x - np.max(x)
        e = np.exp(x)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            break
        p = e / s

    final_f = actual_score_fft(p)
    if np.isfinite(final_f) and final_f < best_f:
        best_f = final_f
        best_p = p.copy()
    return best_p, best_f


def build_conv_matrix(f):
    n = f.size
    rows = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.zeros((rows, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(rows):
        lo = k - n + 1
        if lo < 0:
            lo = 0
        hi = k
        if hi >= n:
            hi = n - 1
        if lo <= hi:
            A[k, lo:hi + 1] = f[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]
    return A


def line_search_params(f, g, S0):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_h = f.copy()
    best_sc = max_conv(f)
    best_t = 0.0

    coarse = (0.0, 0.01, 0.02, 0.03, 0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.18, 0.25,
              0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75, 0.85, 0.95, 1.00,
              1.05, 1.10, 1.15, 1.20)
    for t in coarse:
        if abs(t) < 1e-15:
            sc = best_sc
            h = f
        else:
            h = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            sh = float(np.sum(h))
            if sh <= 1e-300:
                continue
            h = h * (S0 / sh)
            sc = max_conv(h)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_t = float(t)

    if 0.0 < best_t < 1.2:
        lo = max(0.0, best_t - 0.05)
        hi = min(1.2, best_t + 0.05)
        for r in range(11):
            t = lo + (hi - lo) * r / 10.0
            if abs(t - best_t) < 1e-7:
                continue
            h = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            sh = float(np.sum(h))
            if sh <= 1e-300:
                continue
            h = h * (S0 / sh)
            sc = max_conv(h)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_t = float(t)

    return best_h, best_sc, best_t


def lp_improve(p, budget_s, seed=0):
    if scipy_opt is None:
        return p, actual_score_fft(p)

    p = soft_normalize(p, floor=1e-12)
    n = p.size
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if n < 8 or n > 1100 or budget_s < 40.0:
        return p, actual_score_fft(p)

    S0 = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * n))
    f = p * S0
    M = max_conv(f)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 1e-300:
        return p, actual_score_fft(p)

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_score = float(M)
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + max(0.0, budget_s - 25.0)

    if n <= 512:
        max_solves = 35
    elif n <= 800:
        max_solves = 22
    else:
        max_solves = 14

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    ub = 10.0 * S0
    bounds = [(0.0, ub) for _ in range(n)]

    it = 0
    stall = 0
    relax = 1.0

    while time.monotonic() < end and it < max_solves:
        it += 1
        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 10.0:
            break

        A = build_conv_matrix(f)
        b = np.full(2 * n - 1, M * relax, dtype=np.float64)

        if it % 4 == 0:
            cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
            cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) - 0.015 * noise

        tl = min(45.0, max(3.0, rem * 0.35))
        res = None
        try:
            res = scipy_opt.linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": float(tl)},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False) or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        sg = float(np.sum(g))
        if not np.isfinite(sg) or sg <= 1e-12:
            break

        # If a perturbed objective gives very low total mass, it is usually not useful.
        if sg < 0.75 * S0 and (it % 4) != 0:
            stall += 1
            if stall >= 6:
                break
            continue

        g = g * (S0 / sg)
        h, sc, _ = line_search_params(f, g, S0)

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_f = h.copy()
            f = h.copy()
            M = best_score
            relax = 1.0
            stall = 0
            print("LP improve n=%d it=%d score=%.6f" % (n, it, best_score))
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall == 2:
                relax = min(1.25, relax * 1.08)
            elif stall >= 6:
                break

    out = best_f / S0
    out = normalize_nonneg(out, floor=0.0)
    out_score = actual_score_fft(out)
    if not np.isfinite(out_score):
        out_score = best_score
    return out, out_score


class LBFGSProblem:
    def __init__(self, beta):
        self.beta = float(beta)
        self._f = None
        self._g = None
        self._x = None

    def _compute(self, x):
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if x.size == 0:
            self._f = np.inf
            self._g = np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
            self._x = x
            return
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
            x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=60.0, neginf=-60.0)
        x = x - float(np.mean(x))
        x = x - float(np.max(x))
        e = np.exp(x)
        s = float(np.sum(e))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            a = np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
        else:
            a = e / s

        n = a.size
        c = fft_conv(a)
        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if maxc <= 1e-300:
            self._f = 0.0
            self._g = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            self._x = x
            return

        if self.beta <= 1.0:
            w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
            lse = maxc
        else:
            z = self.beta * (c / maxc - 1.0)
            z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
            expz = np.exp(z)
            ssum = float(np.sum(expz))
            if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 1e-300:
                w = np.zeros_like(c)
                w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                lse = maxc
            else:
                w = expz / ssum
                lse = maxc * (1.0 + float(np.log(ssum)) / self.beta)

        fval = float(2.0 * n * lse)
        b = grad_convolution(a, w)
        grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * b
        dot_pg = float(np.dot(a, grad_p))
        grad_x = a * (grad_p - dot_pg)
        grad_x = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_x), grad_x, 0.0)
        self._f = fval
        self._g = grad_x
        self._x = x

    def fun(self, x):
        xp = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if self._x is None or not np.array_equal(self._x, xp):
            self._compute(xp)
        return self._f

    def jac(self, x):
        xp = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if self._x is None or not np.array_equal(self._x, xp):
            self._compute(xp)
        return self._g


def lbfgs_polish(p, budget_s, beta=50.0, seed=0):
    if scipy_opt is None:
        return p, actual_score_fft(p)
    p = soft_normalize(p, floor=1e-12)
    n = p.size
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if n < 4 or n > 2500 or budget_s < 25.0:
        return p, actual_score_fft(p)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_f = actual_score_fft(p)
    x = np.log(p)
    x -= x.mean()
    x = np.clip(x, -60.0, 60.0)
    x -= x.mean()

    prob = LBFGSProblem(beta)
    start = time.monotonic()
    end = start + budget_s

    if n <= 256:
        max_iter = 12
    elif n <= 512:
        max_iter = 8
    elif n <= 1024:
        max_iter = 5
    else:
        max_iter = 3

    while time.monotonic() < end - 5.0:
        try:
            res = scipy_opt.minimize(
                prob.fun,
                x,
                jac=prob.jac,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": int(max_iter), "maxls": int(10), "gtol": 1e-8},
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
            xnew = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if np.all(np.isfinite(xnew)):
                xnew = xnew - np.mean(xnew)
                xnew = xnew - np.max(xnew)
                e = np.exp(xnew)
                s = float(np.sum(e))
                if np.isfinite(s) and s > 1e-300:
                    pnew = e / s
                    fnew = actual_score_fft(pnew)
                    if np.isfinite(fnew) and fnew < best_f:
                        best_f = fnew
                        best_p = pnew.copy()
                    x = xnew
        if hasattr(res, "status") and getattr(res, "status", 0) == 0:
            # Strong convergence: still allow a short restart if time remains.
            pass

    return best_p, best_f


def best_trim_sequence(a, max_margin=48, max_evals=2500):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return a, np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = a.size
    if n < 6:
        sc = actual_score_fft(a)
        return a, sc

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = actual_score_fft(a)
    evals = 1

    max_margin = int(min(int(max_margin), n // 3))
    if max_margin < 1:
        return best_a, best_sc
    max_margin = min(max_margin, int(np.sqrt(max(1.0, float(max_evals) / 2.0))))

    for i in range(max_margin + 1):
        if evals >= max_evals:
            break
        for j in range(max_margin + 1):
            if evals >= max_evals:
                break
            if i == 0 and j == 0:
                continue
            new_len = n - i - j
            if new_len < 6:
                continue
            cand = a[i:n - j]
            sc = actual_score_fft(cand)
            evals += 1
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = cand.copy()

    improved = True
    while improved and best_a.size > 6:
        improved = False
        base = best_sc
        for cand in (best_a[1:], best_a[:-1]):
            if cand.size < 6:
                continue
            sc = actual_score_fft(cand)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < base - 1e-12:
                best_a = cand
                best_sc = float(sc)
                improved = True
                break

    return best_a, best_sc


def scale_output(p, target_sum=10.0):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(16, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    a = a * (float(target_sum) / s)
    amax = float(np.max(a))
    if amax > 1000.0:
        a = a * (1000.0 / amax)
    s2 = float(np.sum(a))
    if s2 < 0.01:
        a = a * (0.01 / s2)
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def try_global_eval(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(list(seq))
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    return val if np.isfinite(val) else np.inf


def main_search(seed, budget_s):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total_deadline = t0 + max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(30.0, float(budget_s) - 75.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    best_by_n = {}

    # Use any supplied known-good constructions, and immediately try trimmed windows.
    for gname in ("height_sequence_1", "height_sequence_2"):
        h = globals().get(gname, None)
        if h is None:
            continue
        try:
            hp = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            hp = np.clip(hp, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if hp.size == 0:
                continue
            p0 = soft_normalize(hp, floor=1e-12)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p0, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)
            pt, ft = best_trim_sequence(p0, max_margin=64, max_evals=8000)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(pt, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, ft)
            print("loaded %s len=%d score=%.6f" % (gname, hp.size, best_score))
        except Exception as ex:
            print("failed loading %s: %s" % (gname, repr(ex)))

    # Structured baselines.  Include lengths around the supplied best if available.
    base_lengths = [32, 48, 64, 96, 128, 160, 192, 256, 320, 384, 448, 512,
                    640, 768, 1024, 1536, 2048, 3072, 4096]
    if best_p is not None:
        bn = int(best_p.size)
        for fac in (0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 2.0):
            base_lengths.append(int(round(bn * fac)))
    base_lengths = sorted(set(x for x in base_lengths if 16 <= x <= 4096))

    for n in base_lengths:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 15.0:
            break
        if n <= 1024:
            kinds = ["greedy", "power45", "power50", "power55", "mix50", "geom1", "geom2"]
        else:
            kinds = ["greedy", "power50"]
        for kind in kinds:
            if deadline - time.monotonic() < 10.0:
                break
            p = prepare_start(n, kind, rng)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)

        if best_p is not None:
            blen = int(best_p.size)
            if blen >= 16 and 0.45 * blen <= n <= 2.2 * blen:
                pr = resample_cdf(best_p, n)
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(pr, best_p, best_score, best_by_n)

    print("baseline best internal score:", best_score)

    # Short Adam screening of promising lengths.
    screen_budget = min(240.0, max(80.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.12))
    selected = select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, 8)
    if selected:
        per = screen_budget / float(len(selected))
        for idx, item in enumerate(selected):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 30.0:
                break
            sc, n, p = item
            alloc = min(per, max(12.0, rem / 3.0))
            if alloc < 10.0:
                break
            p2, f2 = adam_v2(p, alloc, beta=100.0, lr=0.040, hard_frac=0.0, seed=int(seed) + 17 + n)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p2, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f2)
        print("after Adam screen best:", best_score)

    # LP phase, plus one reserved longer/other length to avoid premature specialization.
    if scipy_opt is not None:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 220.0:
            lp_candidates = select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, 2, max_len=1024)
            seen_n = set(int(x[1]) for x in lp_candidates)

            # Reserve one additional length: preferably a longer one below 1024.
            reserve = None
            min_reserve = max(192, int(best_p.size / 2) if best_p is not None else 192)
            for n in sorted(best_by_n.keys(), reverse=True):
                n = int(n)
                if n <= 1024 and n >= min_reserve and n not in seen_n:
                    reserve = (float(best_by_n[n][0]), n, best_by_n[n][1])
                    break
            if reserve is not None:
                lp_candidates.append(reserve)

            lp_candidates = lp_candidates[:3]
            total_lp = min(900.0, rem * 0.55)
            fracs = (0.45, 0.30, 0.25)
            for idx, item in enumerate(lp_candidates):
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 120.0:
                    break
                sc, n, p = item
                frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.2
                alloc = min(total_lp * frac, rem - 60.0)
                if alloc < 70.0:
                    continue
                print("LP candidate n=%d current=%.6f alloc=%.0f" % (n, sc, alloc))
                p_out, f_out = lp_improve(p, alloc, seed=int(seed) + 1000 + n)
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p_out, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f_out)
            print("after LP best:", best_score)
    else:
        # If SciPy/HI-GHS isn't available, spend more Adam budget.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 200.0:
            tops = select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, 5)
            for idx, item in enumerate(tops):
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 120.0:
                    break
                sc, n, p = item
                alloc = min(220.0, max(80.0, rem * 0.25))
                p1, f1 = adam_v2(p, alloc, beta=80.0, lr=0.038, hard_frac=0.0, seed=int(seed) + 70 + n)
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p1, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f1)
                p2 = random_perturb(p1, rng, 0.07)
                p3, f3 = adam_v2(p2, alloc * 0.6, beta=150.0, lr=0.028, hard_frac=0.05, seed=int(seed) + 91 + n)
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p3, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f3)
        print("after fallback Adam best:", best_score)

    # Post-LP polish: sharper smoothed max and hard active-set Adam, with random restarts.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0:
        total_post = min(360.0, rem * 0.45)
        tops = select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, 3)
        if tops:
            per = total_post / float(len(tops))
            for idx, item in enumerate(tops):
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 90.0:
                    break
                sc, n, p = item
                alloc = min(per, rem - 40.0)
                if alloc < 50.0:
                    break
                p1, f1 = adam_v2(p, alloc * 0.45, beta=80.0, lr=0.035, seed=int(seed) + 201 + n)
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p1, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f1)
                p2, f2 = adam_v2(p1, alloc * 0.30, hard_frac=0.03, lr=0.025, seed=int(seed) + 211 + n)
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p2, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f2)
                if rem > 60.0:
                    p3 = random_perturb(p2, rng, 0.06)
                    p4, f4 = adam_v2(p3, alloc * 0.25, hard_frac=0.08, lr=0.020, seed=int(seed) + 221 + n)
                    best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p4, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f4)
        print("after post-LP Adam best:", best_score)

    # Optional L-BFGS polish.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if scipy_opt is not None and rem > 120.0:
        total_lb = min(220.0, rem * 0.40)
        tops = select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, 2, max_len=1300)
        for idx, item in enumerate(tops):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 80.0:
                break
            sc, n, p = item
            alloc = min(total_lb / max(1, len(tops)), rem - 40.0)
            if alloc < 45.0:
                break
            p1, f1 = lbfgs_polish(p, alloc, beta=45.0, seed=int(seed) + 301 + n)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p1, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f1)
            if rem > 60.0:
                p2 = random_perturb(p, rng, 0.03)
                p3, f3 = lbfgs_polish(p2, max(25.0, alloc * 0.4), beta=70.0, seed=int(seed) + 311 + n)
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p3, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f3)
        print("after L-BFGS best:", best_score)

    # Trim prefix/suffix margins of the best candidates.  This can reduce n directly.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 35.0:
        tops = select_top(best_by_n, best_p, best_score, 5)
        for idx, item in enumerate(tops):
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 30.0:
                break
            sc, n, p = item
            if n <= 512:
                mm, me = 64, 3000
            elif n <= 1024:
                mm, me = 48, 2200
            elif n <= 2048:
                mm, me = 36, 1400
            else:
                mm, me = 28, 900
            pt, ft = best_trim_sequence(p, max_margin=mm, max_evals=me)
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(pt, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, ft)
        print("after trimming best:", best_score)

    # Final short local improvement, then trim again.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_p is not None and rem > 55.0:
        p0 = soft_normalize(best_p, floor=1e-12)
        p1, f1 = adam_v2(p0, min(35.0, rem - 25.0), beta=120.0, lr=0.025, hard_frac=0.0, seed=int(seed) + 999)
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(p1, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, f1)
        pt, ft = best_trim_sequence(p1, max_margin=32, max_evals=1200)
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(pt, best_p, best_score, best_by_n, ft)
        print("after final short polish best:", best_score)

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = greedy_normalized(384)
        best_score = actual_score_fft(best_p)

    # Validate the strongest internal candidates against the authoritative evaluator.
    candidates = []
    if best_p is not None:
        candidates.append(best_p)

    items = []
    for n, rec in best_by_n.items():
        items.append((float(rec[0]), int(n), rec[1]))
    items.sort(key=tuple_score_key)
    for _, _, p in items[:8]:
        candidates.append(p)

    best_final_p = best_p
    best_final_val = np.inf
    hard_end = total_deadline - 5.0

    for cand in candidates:
        if time.monotonic() >= hard_end:
            break
        seq = scale_output(cand, 10.0)
        val = try_global_eval(seq)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            print("validated candidate len=%d eval=%.9f" % (len(seq), val))
            if val < best_final_val:
                best_final_val = val
                best_final_p = cand

    try:
        final_seq = scale_output(best_final_p, 10.0)
    except Exception:
        final_seq = scale_output(greedy_normalized(384), 10.0)

    return final_seq


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    """Entry point: return a list of non-negative floats."""
    try:
        return main_search(int(seed), float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        return scale_output(greedy_normalized(384), 10.0)
```
